## Summary: Wingman Enable Command and Settings Loading/Saving

I've thoroughly analyzed the codebase and found all the relevant components. Here's a complete overview:

### 1. **Wingman Enable Command Implementation**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` (lines 83-116)

```go
func newWingmanEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
	return &cobra.Command{
		Use:   "enable",
		Short: "Enable wingman auto-review",
		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run from within a git repository.")
				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
			}

			s, err := settings.Load()
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
			}

			if !s.Enabled {
				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Entire is not enabled. Run 'entire enable' first.")
				return NewSilentError(errors.New("entire not enabled"))
			}

			if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
				s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
			}
			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": true}

			if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
			}

			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman enabled. Code changes will be automatically reviewed after agent turns.")
			return nil
		},
	}
}
```

**Key Flow:**
1. Verify Git repository exists
2. Load settings (which merges `.entire/settings.json` + `.entire/settings.local.json`)
3. Check if Entire is enabled
4. Update `s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = {enabled: true}`
5. Save settings back to `.entire/settings.json`

### 2. **Settings Load/Save Functions**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`

#### **EntireSettings Struct** (lines 28-55)
```go
type EntireSettings struct {
	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
	
	// Agent is the name of the agent (e.g., "claude-code", "amp")
	Agent string `json:"agent,omitempty"`
	
	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active
	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
	
	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
	
	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error)
	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
	
	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
	
	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics
	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
}
```

#### **Load Function** (lines 57-94)
This is the **main entry point** for loading settings:

```go
func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
	// Get absolute paths for settings files
	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
	if err != nil {
		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
	}
	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
	if err != nil {
		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
	}

	// Load base settings
	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
	}

	// Apply local overrides if they exist
	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec
	if err != nil {
		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
		}
		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
	} else {
		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
		}
	}

	applyDefaults(settings)
	return settings, nil
}
```

**Key Behavior:**
- Loads `.entire/settings.json` first with defaults if missing
- Merges `.entire/settings.local.json` if it exists (overrides project settings)
- Returns defaults if neither file exists
- Works from any subdirectory via `paths.AbsPath()`

#### **LoadFromFile Function** (lines 96-127)
```go
// Loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides
func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
	return loadFromFile(filePath)
}
```

#### **mergeJSON Function** (lines 129-222)
Carefully merges local settings over project settings using selective field override logic:

- For string fields (`strategy`, `agent`, `log_level`): override if present and non-empty
- For bool fields (`enabled`, `local_dev`): override if present
- For `strategy_options`: merge map entries (preserve existing keys not in override)
- For `telemetry`: override if present
- Uses strict JSON decoding (rejects unknown keys)

#### **Save Function** (lines 298-301)
```go
func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
}
```

Saves to `.entire/settings.json` with 0o644 permissions.

#### **SaveLocal Function** (lines 303-306)
```go
func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
}
```

Saves to `.entire/settings.local.json` with 0o644 permissions.

#### **saveToFile Function** (lines 308-332)
```go
func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
	// Get absolute path for the file
	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
	if err != nil {
		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
	}

	// Ensure directory exists
	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
	}

	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
	}

	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

### 3. **Settings File Structure**

#### **`.entire/settings.json` (Project Settings)**
Example from the git diff:
```json
{
  "strategy": "manual-commit",
  "enabled": true,
  "local_dev": true,
  "strategy_options": {
    "wingman": {
      "enabled": true
    }
  }
}
```

#### **`.entire/settings.local.json` (Local Overrides)**
Omitted if no local overrides exist. When present, fields override corresponding project settings:
```json
{
  "strategy_options": {
    "wingman": {
      "enabled": false
    }
  }
}
```

### 4. **Existing --local Flag Pattern**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`

#### **Enable Command Flags** (lines 109-119)
```go
cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useLocalSettings, "local", false, "Write settings to settings.local.json instead of settings.json")
cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write settings to settings.json even if it already exists")
```

#### **Flag Validation** (lines 648-654)
```go
func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
	if useLocal && useProject {
		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
	}
	return nil
}
```

#### **Target Determination Logic** (lines 656-680)
```go
func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
	if useLocal {
		return true, false
	}

	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
	if useProject {
		return false, false
	}

	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
		return true, true
	}

	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
	return false, false
}
```

**Behavior:**
- `--local`: Always write to `settings.local.json`, no notification
- `--project`: Always write to `settings.json`, no notification
- No flag + settings.json exists: Write to `settings.local.json` + show notification
- No flag + settings.json doesn't exist: Write to `settings.json`, no notification

### 5. **Config.go Wrapper Functions**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/config.go` (lines 24-53)

The `cli` package wraps settings functions to avoid import cycles:

```go
// Aliases to avoid shadowing the settings package
const (
	EntireSettingsFile      = settings.EntireSettingsFile
	EntireSettingsLocalFile = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile
)

type EntireSettings = settings.EntireSettings

// LoadEntireSettings loads settings (merges project + local)
func LoadEntireSettings() (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {
	s, err := settings.Load()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading settings: %w", err)
	}
	return s, nil
}

// SaveEntireSettings saves to .entire/settings.json
func SaveEntireSettings(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
	if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("saving settings: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// SaveEntireSettingsLocal saves to .entire/settings.local.json
func SaveEntireSettingsLocal(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
	if err := settings.SaveLocal(s); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("saving local settings: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

### 6. **Wingman Enable Check Functions**

**Location:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go` (lines 256-280)

```go
// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in settings
// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing
func IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
	s, err := Load()
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	return s.IsWingmanEnabled()
}

// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in this settings instance
func (s *EntireSettings) IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
		return false
	}
	wingmanOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["wingman"].(map[string]any)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	enabled, ok := wingmanOpts["enabled"].(bool)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	return enabled
}
```

// saveWingmanStateDirect writes wingman state directly without chdir
func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
		return
	}
	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
}

### 9. **Key Implementation Details**

#### **Settings Merging Strategy**
- Project settings are loaded first as base
- Local settings are merged on top (non-zero values override)
- `strategy_options` map is merged (new keys added, existing keys overridden)

#### **File Paths**
- Constants: `EntireSettingsFile` = `".entire/settings.json"`, `EntireSettingsLocalFile` = `".entire/settings.json"`
- Uses `paths.AbsPath()` for repo-root-relative resolution
- Falls back to relative paths if AbsPath fails

#### **Error Handling**
- Missing files return defaults (enabled, strategy="manual-commit")
- Unknown keys cause parse errors
- Both JSON files strictly validated with `DisallowUnknownFields()`

#### **Wingman-Specific Settings**
Stored in `strategy_options.wingman`:
```json
{
  "strategy_options": {
    "wingman": {
      "enabled": true
    }
  }
}
```

---

### 10. **How Wingman Enable Integrates with Entire Enable**

The `wingman enable` command:
1. Requires `entire enable` to have been run first (checks `s.Enabled`)
2. Doesn't create new settings, only modifies existing ones
3. Uses the standard settings loading/saving pipeline
4. Saves to the **same file** that contains project configuration

This is different from the `entire enable` command, which:
- Uses `--local` / `--project` flags to choose which file to write
- Applies logic to auto-redirect to local if project settings exist
- Shows user notifications about file choices

   189→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   190→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   191→	// Prevent infinite recursion: if we're inside a wingman auto-apply,
   192→	// don't trigger another review. The env var is set by triggerAutoApply.
   193→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   194→		return
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   198→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   199→
   200→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
   201→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman trigger evaluating",
   202→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   203→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   204→	)
   205→
   206→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists for the current session
   207→	if shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, payload.SessionID) {
   208→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: pending review exists for current session")
   209→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   210→		return
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
   214→	// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.
   215→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   216→	if !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {
   217→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: review already in progress")
   218→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review in progress, skipping\n")
   219→		return
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   223→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   224→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   225→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   226→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   227→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   228→
   229→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   230→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   231→		logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman skipped: dedup hash match",
   232→			slog.String("files_hash", filesHash[:12]),
   233→		)
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   235→		return
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   239→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   240→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   241→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman captured base commit",
   242→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   243→	)
   244→
   245→	// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,
   246→	// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with
   247→	// many files or long prompts.
   248→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to marshal payload", slog.Any("error", err))
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   252→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   253→		return
   254→	}
   255→	payloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "wingman-payload.json")
   256→	//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets
   257→	if err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {
   258→		logging.Error(logCtx, "wingman failed to write payload file", slog.Any("error", err))
   259→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\n", err)
   260→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   261→		return
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file
   265→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath)
   266→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman review spawned",
   267→		slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
   268→		slog.String("base_commit", payload.BaseCommit),
   269→		slog.Int("file_count", totalFiles),
   270→	)
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// triggerWingmanFromCommit builds a wingman payload from the HEAD commit and
   275→// triggers a review. Used by the git post-commit hook for manual-commit strategy
   276→// where files are committed by the user (not by SaveChanges).
   277→func triggerWingmanFromCommit() {
   278→	// Prevent infinite recursion: skip if inside wingman auto-apply
   279→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   280→		return
   281→	}
   282→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   283→		return
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	head := resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   292→	if head == "" {
   293→		return
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Get changed files from the commit
   297→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
   298→	defer cancel()
   299→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   300→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-status", "-r", head)
   301→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   302→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   303→	if err != nil || len(out) == 0 {
   304→		return
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	var modified, newFiles, deleted []string
   308→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n") {
   309→		if len(line) < 3 {
   310→			continue
   311→		}
   312→		status := line[0]
   313→		file := strings.TrimSpace(line[1:])
   314→		switch status {
   315→		case 'M':
   316→			modified = append(modified, file)
   317→		case 'A':
   318→			newFiles = append(newFiles, file)
   319→		case 'D':
   320→			deleted = append(deleted, file)
   321→		}
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	if len(modified)+len(newFiles)+len(deleted) == 0 {
   325→		return
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Get commit message
   329→	//nolint:gosec // G204: head is from git rev-parse, not user input
   330→	msgCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%B", head)
   331→	msgCmd.Dir = repoRoot
   332→	msgOut, _ := msgCmd.Output() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort commit message
   333→	commitMessage := strings.TrimSpace(string(msgOut))
   334→
   335→	sessionID := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
   336→
   337→	triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   338→		SessionID:     sessionID,
   339→		RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   340→		ModifiedFiles: modified,
   341→		NewFiles:      newFiles,
   342→		DeletedFiles:  deleted,
   343→		CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   344→	})
   345→}
   346→
   347→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   348→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   349→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   350→
   351→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   352→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   353→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   354→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   355→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   356→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   357→	if err != nil {
   358→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   359→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   360→			return false
   361→		}
   362→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   363→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   364→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   366→			return false
   367→		}
   368→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   369→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   370→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   371→		// Retry the atomic create
   372→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   373→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   374→		if err != nil {
   375→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   376→			return false
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   380→	_ = f.Close()
   381→	return true
   382→}
   383→
   384→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   385→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   386→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
   387→	defer cancel()
   388→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   389→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   390→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   391→	if err != nil {
   392→		return ""
   393→	}
   394→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   395→}
   396→
   397→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   398→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   399→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   400→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   401→	copy(sorted, files)
   402→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   403→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   404→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   405→}
   406→
   407→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   408→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   409→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   410→	if err != nil {
   411→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   415→	if err != nil {
   416→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	var state WingmanState
   420→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   421→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   422→	}
   423→	return &state, nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   427→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   428→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   429→	if err != nil {
   430→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   434→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   435→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   439→	if err != nil {
   440→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   444→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   445→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   446→	}
   447→	return nil
   448→}
   449→
   450→// loadWingmanStateDirect reads the wingman state file directly from a known
   451→// path under repoRoot. Returns nil if the file doesn't exist or can't be parsed.
   452→func loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string) *WingmanState {
   453→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   454→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative constant
   455→	if err != nil {
   456→		return nil
   457→	}
   458→	var state WingmanState
   459→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   460→		return nil
   461→	}
   462→	return &state
   463→}
   464→
   465→// newWingmanApplyCmd returns a hidden subcommand used by the stop hook to
   466→// apply a pending REVIEW.md via claude --continue in a detached subprocess.
   467→func newWingmanApplyCmd() *cobra.Command {
   468→	return &cobra.Command{
   469→		Use:    "__apply",
   470→		Hidden: true,
   471→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
   472→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   473→			return runWingmanApply(args[0])
   474→		},
   475→	}
   476→}
   477→
   478→// shouldSkipPendingReview checks whether a pending REVIEW.md should prevent
   479→// a new review from being triggered. It cleans up stale/orphaned reviews.
   480→//
   481→// Returns true only when REVIEW.md exists AND belongs to the current session
   482→// AND is younger than wingmanStaleReviewTTL.
   483→func shouldSkipPendingReview(repoRoot, currentSessionID string) bool {
   484→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   485→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err != nil {
   486→		return false // No REVIEW.md, don't skip
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// REVIEW.md exists — check state to determine if it's current or stale
   490→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   491→	if state == nil {
   492→		// Orphan: REVIEW.md without state file — clean up
   493→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   494→		return false
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	if state.SessionID != currentSessionID {
   498→		// Different session — stale review, clean up
   499→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   500→		return false
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	if time.Since(state.ReviewedAt) > wingmanStaleReviewTTL {
   504→		// Same session but too old — clean up
   505→		_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   506→		return false
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	return true // Current session, fresh review — skip
   510→}

   238→	}
   239→
   240→	filtered := wingmanStripGitEnv(env)
   241→
   242→	for _, e := range filtered {
   243→		if strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   244→			t.Errorf("GIT_ variable should be stripped: %s", e)
   245→		}
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	if len(filtered) != 3 {
   249→		t.Errorf("expected 3 non-GIT vars, got %d", len(filtered))
   250→	}
   251→}

   253→func TestIsWingmanEnabled_Settings(t *testing.T) {
   254→	t.Parallel()
   255→
   256→	tests := []struct {
   257→		name     string
   258→		options  map[string]any
   259→		expected bool
   260→	}{
   261→		{
   262→			name:     "nil options",
   263→			options:  nil,
   264→			expected: false,
   265→		},
   266→		{
   267→			name:     "empty options",

   269→			expected: false,
   270→		},
   271→		{

   273→			options:  map[string]any{"summarize": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   274→			expected: false,
   275→		},
   276→		{
   277→			name:     "wingman enabled",
   278→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": true}},
   279→			expected: true,
   280→		},
   281→		{
   282→			name:     "wingman disabled",
   283→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": false}},
   284→			expected: false,
   285→		},
   286→		{
   287→			name:     "wingman wrong type",
   288→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": "invalid"},
   289→			expected: false,
   290→		},
   291→		{
   292→			name:     "wingman enabled wrong type",
   293→			options:  map[string]any{"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": "yes"}},
   294→			expected: false,
   295→		},
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	for _, tt := range tests {

   303→			}
   304→			if got := s.IsWingmanEnabled(); got != tt.expected {
   305→				t.Errorf("IsWingmanEnabled() = %v, want %v", got, tt.expected)
   306→			}
   307→		})
   308→	}
   309→}

   392→	})

    46→func newEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
    47→	var localDev bool
    48→	var ignoreUntracked bool
    49→	var useLocalSettings bool
    50→	var useProjectSettings bool
    51→	var agentName string
    52→	var strategyFlag string
    53→	var forceHooks bool
    54→	var skipPushSessions bool
    55→	var telemetry bool
    56→
    57→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    58→		Use:   "enable",
    59→		Short: "Enable Entire in current project",
    60→		Long: `Enable Entire with session tracking for your AI agent workflows.
    61→
    62→Uses the manual-commit strategy by default. To use a different strategy:
    63→
    64→  entire enable --strategy auto-commit
    65→
    66→Strategies: manual-commit (default), auto-commit`,
    67→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    68→			// Check if we're in a git repository first - this is a prerequisite error,
    69→			// not a usage error, so we silence Cobra's output and use SilentError
    70→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go
    71→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    72→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run 'entire enable' from within a git repository.")
    73→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
    74→			}
    75→
    76→			if err := validateSetupFlags(useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings); err != nil {
    77→				return err
    78→			}
    79→
    80→			// Warn if repo has no commits yet
    81→			if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
    82→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Note: This repository has no commits yet. Entire will be configured, but")
    83→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "session checkpoints won't work until you create your first commit.")
    84→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout())
    85→			}
    86→
    87→			// Non-interactive mode if --agent flag is provided
    88→			if cmd.Flags().Changed("agent") && agentName == "" {
    89→				printMissingAgentError(cmd.ErrOrStderr())
    90→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("missing agent name"))
    91→			}
    92→
    93→			if agentName != "" {
    94→				ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentName(agentName))
    95→				if err != nil {
    96→					printWrongAgentError(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), agentName)
    97→					return NewSilentError(errors.New("wrong agent name"))
    98→				}
    99→				return setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), ag, strategyFlag, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
   100→			}
   101→			// If strategy is specified via flag, skip interactive selection
   102→			if strategyFlag != "" {
   103→				return runEnableWithStrategy(cmd.OutOrStdout(), strategyFlag, localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
   104→			}
   105→			return runEnableInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
   106→		},
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&localDev, "local-dev", false, "Use go run instead of entire binary for hooks")
   110→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("local-dev") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above

     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// DefaultStrategyName is the default strategy when none is configured.
    18→// This is duplicated here to avoid importing the strategy package (which would create a cycle).
    19→const DefaultStrategyName = "manual-commit"
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    23→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    24→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    25→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    29→type EntireSettings struct {
    30→	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
    31→	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
    32→
    33→	// Agent is the name of the agent (e.g., "claude-code", "amp")
    34→	Agent string `json:"agent,omitempty"`
    35→
    36→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    37→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    38→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    39→
    40→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    41→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    42→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    45→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    46→	// Defaults to "info".
    47→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    48→
    49→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    50→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    51→
    52→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    53→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    54→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    55→}
    56→
    57→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    58→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    59→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    60→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    61→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    62→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    63→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    66→	}
    67→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Load base settings
    73→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    79→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    82→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    83→		}
    84→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    85→	} else {
    86→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    87→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    88→		}
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	applyDefaults(settings)
    92→
    93→	return settings, nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    97→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    98→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    99→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   100→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
   101→}
   102→
   103→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
   104→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
   105→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   106→	settings := &EntireSettings{
   107→		Strategy: DefaultStrategyName,
   108→		Enabled:  true, // Default to enabled
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   114→			return settings, nil
   115→		}
   116→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   120→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   121→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   122→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   123→	}
   124→	applyDefaults(settings)
   125→
   126→	return settings, nil
   127→}
   128→
   129→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   130→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   131→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   132→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   133→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   134→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   135→	var temp EntireSettings
   136→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   137→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   141→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   142→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   143→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// Override strategy if present and non-empty
   147→	if strategyRaw, ok := raw["strategy"]; ok {
   148→		var s string
   149→		if err := json.Unmarshal(strategyRaw, &s); err != nil {
   150→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy field: %w", err)
   151→		}
   152→		if s != "" {
   153→			settings.Strategy = s
   154→		}
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Override agent if present and non-empty
   158→	if agentRaw, ok := raw["agent"]; ok {
   159→		var a string
   160→		if err := json.Unmarshal(agentRaw, &a); err != nil {
   161→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing agent field: %w", err)
   162→		}
   163→		if a != "" {
   164→			settings.Agent = a
   165→		}
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Override enabled if present
   169→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   170→		var e bool
   171→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   172→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   173→		}
   174→		settings.Enabled = e
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Override local_dev if present
   178→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   179→		var ld bool
   180→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   181→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   182→		}
   183→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   187→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   188→		var ll string
   189→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   190→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   191→		}
   192→		if ll != "" {
   193→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   194→		}
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   198→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   199→		var opts map[string]any
   200→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   201→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   202→		}
   203→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   204→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   205→		} else {
   206→			for k, v := range opts {
   207→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   208→			}
   209→		}
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Override telemetry if present
   213→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   214→		var t bool
   215→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   216→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   217→		}
   218→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	return nil
   222→}
   223→
   224→func applyDefaults(settings *EntireSettings) {
   225→	if settings.Strategy == "" {
   226→		settings.Strategy = DefaultStrategyName
   227→	}
   228→}
   229→
   230→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   231→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   232→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   233→	settings, err := Load()
   234→	if err != nil {
   235→		return false
   236→	}
   237→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   238→}
   239→
   240→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   241→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   242→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   243→		return false
   244→	}
   245→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   246→	if !ok {
   247→		return false
   248→	}
   249→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   250→	if !ok {
   251→		return false
   252→	}
   253→	return enabled
   254→}
   255→
   256→// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in settings.
   257→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   258→func IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
   259→	s, err := Load()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return false
   262→	}
   263→	return s.IsWingmanEnabled()
   264→}
   265→
   266→// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in this settings instance.
   267→func (s *EntireSettings) IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
   268→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   269→		return false
   270→	}
   271→	wingmanOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["wingman"].(map[string]any)
   272→	if !ok {
   273→		return false
   274→	}
   275→	enabled, ok := wingmanOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   276→	if !ok {
   277→		return false
   278→	}
   279→	return enabled
   280→}
   281→
   282→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   283→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   284→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   285→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   286→		return false
   287→	}
   288→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   289→	if !exists {
   290→		return false
   291→	}
   292→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   293→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   294→	}
   295→	return false
   296→}
   297→
   298→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   299→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   300→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   301→}
   302→
   303→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   304→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   305→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   306→}
   307→
   308→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   309→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   310→	// Get absolute path for the file
   311→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Ensure directory exists
   317→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   318→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   319→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   323→	if err != nil {
   324→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   328→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   329→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   330→	}
   331→	return nil
   332→}
   333→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go",
  "old_string": "// loadSettingsTarget loads settings from the appropriate file based on the --local flag.\n// When local is true, loads from settings.local.json only (without merging).\n// When local is false, loads the merged settings (project + local).\nfunc loadSettingsTarget(local bool) (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {\n\tif !local {\n\t\treturn settings.Load()\n\t}\n\tabsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tabsPath = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile\n\t}\n\treturn settings.LoadFromFile(absPath)\n}\n\n// saveSettingsTarget saves settings to the appropriate file based on the --local flag.\nfunc saveSettingsTarget(s *settings.EntireSettings, local bool) error {\n\tif local {\n\t\treturn settings.SaveLocal(s)\n\t}\n\treturn settings.Save(s)\n}",
  "new_string": "// loadSettingsTarget loads settings from the appropriate file based on the --local flag.\n// When local is true, loads from settings.local.json only (without merging).\n// When local is false, loads the merged settings (project + local).\nfunc loadSettingsTarget(local bool) (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {\n\tif !local {\n\t\ts, err := settings.Load()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"loading settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn s, nil\n\t}\n\tabsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tabsPath = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile\n\t}\n\ts, err := settings.LoadFromFile(absPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"loading local settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn s, nil\n}\n\n// saveSettingsTarget saves settings to the appropriate file based on the --local flag.\nfunc saveSettingsTarget(s *settings.EntireSettings, local bool) error {\n\tif local {\n\t\tif err := settings.SaveLocal(s); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"saving local settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"saving settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

All tests pass, lint is clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**`cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`:**
- `newWingmanEnableCmd()` — Added `--local` flag. When set, loads only the local settings file, updates wingman config, and saves to `settings.local.json`. Output message includes "(saved to settings.local.json)" when local is used.
- `newWingmanDisableCmd()` — Added matching `--local` flag for consistency.
- `loadSettingsTarget(local bool)` — New helper that loads merged settings (default) or just the local file (when `--local`). Loading the target file specifically prevents bloating the local file with merged values from the project settings.
- `saveSettingsTarget(s, local bool)` — New helper that saves to the appropriate file.

**`cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go`:**
- `TestLoadSettingsTarget_Local` — Verifies `local=false` returns merged settings while `local=true` returns only local file contents.
- `TestSaveSettingsTarget_Local` — Verifies `local=true` writes to `settings.local.json` and `local=false` writes to `settings.json`.

+
+// loadSettingsTarget loads settings from the appropriate file based on the --local flag.
+// When local is true, loads from settings.local.json only (without merging).
+// When local is false, loads the merged settings (project + local).
+func loadSettingsTarget(local bool) (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {
+	if !local {
+		s, err := settings.Load()
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading settings: %w", err)
+		}
+		return s, nil
+	}
+	absPath, err := paths.AbsPath(settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile)
+	if err != nil {
+		absPath = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile
+	}
+	s, err := settings.LoadFromFile(absPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading local settings: %w", err)
+	}
+	return s, nil
+}
+
+// saveSettingsTarget saves settings to the appropriate file based on the --local flag.
+func saveSettingsTarget(s *settings.EntireSettings, local bool) error {
+	if local {
+		if err := settings.SaveLocal(s); err != nil {
+			return fmt.Errorf("saving local settings: %w", err)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("saving settings: %w", err)
+	}
+	return nil
 }
 
 func newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {

+func TestLoadSettingsTarget_Local(t *testing.T) {
+	// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "init", tmpDir)
+	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to git init: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".entire")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	// Write project settings with wingman disabled
+	projectSettings := `{"strategy": "manual-commit", "enabled": true, "strategy_options": {"wingman": {"enabled": false}}}`
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.json"), []byte(projectSettings), 0o644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	// Write local settings with different strategy
+	localSettings := `{"strategy": "` + strategyDisplayAutoCommit + `"}`
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.local.json"), []byte(localSettings), 0o644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
+
+	t.Run("local=false returns merged settings", func(t *testing.T) {
+		s, err := loadSettingsTarget(false)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+		}
+		// Merged: local overrides project strategy
+		if s.Strategy != strategyDisplayAutoCommit {
+			t.Errorf("Strategy = %q, want %q", s.Strategy, strategyDisplayAutoCommit)
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("local=true returns only local settings", func(t *testing.T) {
+		s, err := loadSettingsTarget(true)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+		}
+		if s.Strategy != strategyDisplayAutoCommit {
+			t.Errorf("Strategy = %q, want %q", s.Strategy, strategyDisplayAutoCommit)
+		}
+		// Local file has no wingman settings
+		if s.IsWingmanEnabled() {
+			t.Error("local settings should not have wingman enabled")
+		}
+	})
+}
+
+func TestSaveSettingsTarget_Local(t *testing.T) {
+	// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel
+	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
+
+	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "init", tmpDir)
+	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to git init: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	entireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".entire")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
+
+	s := &EntireSettings{
+		StrategyOptions: map[string]any{
+			"wingman": map[string]any{"enabled": true},
+		},
+	}
+
+	t.Run("local=true saves to local file", func(t *testing.T) {
+		if err := saveSettingsTarget(s, true); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		localPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.local.json")
+		data, err := os.ReadFile(localPath)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("local settings file should exist: %v", err)
+		}
+		if !strings.Contains(string(data), `"wingman"`) {
+			t.Error("local settings should contain wingman config")
+		}
+
+		// Project file should not exist
+		projectPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.json")
+		if _, err := os.Stat(projectPath); err == nil {
+			t.Error("project settings file should not have been created")
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("local=false saves to project file", func(t *testing.T) {
+		if err := saveSettingsTarget(s, false); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		projectPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, "settings.json")
+		data, err := os.ReadFile(projectPath)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("project settings file should exist: %v", err)
+		}
+		if !strings.Contains(string(data), `"wingman"`) {
+			t.Error("project settings should contain wingman config")
+		}
+	})
+}

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide a comprehensive summary to the user.

## Summary: Agent Hook System Architecture

Based on my thorough review of the codebase, here's how the agent hook system works:

### 1. Hook Registry and Registration (`hook_registry.go`)

**Hook Registry Pattern:**
- Global map: `hookRegistry = map[agent.AgentName]map[string]HookHandlerFunc{}`
- Handlers registered via `RegisterHookHandler(agentName, hookName, handler)`
- Retrieved via `GetHookHandler(agentName, hookName)`

**Registration at Startup (`init()`):**
- Claude Code hooks: `session-start`, `session-end`, `stop`, `user-prompt-submit`, `pre-task`, `post-task`, `post-todo`
- Gemini CLI hooks: `session-start`, `session-end`, `before-tool`, `after-tool`, `before-agent`, `after-agent`, `before-model`, `after-model`, etc.
- Each handler wraps with `IsEnabled()` check before executing

**Hook Command Structure:**
- Hooks are CLI subcommands under `entire hooks <agent-name> <hook-name>`
- Example: `entire hooks claude-code stop`, `entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit`
- Each hook subcommand sets up logging via `initHookLogging()` and captures hook context

### 2. Stop Hook (`commitWithMetadata()`)

**What it does:**
- Fires when Claude Code stops working (end of turn)
- Parses hook input from stdin via agent's `ParseHookInput()` method
- **Core responsibility**: Creates a checkpoint with all session metadata

**Checkpoint Process:**
1. Waits for transcript file to flush to disk (polls for sentinel entry)
2. Copies transcript to `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/full.jsonl`
3. Extracts user prompts → `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/prompt.txt`
4. Extracts last assistant message → `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/summary.txt`
5. Detects file changes (new, modified, deleted)
6. Generates commit message from last user prompt
7. Creates context file → `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/context.md`
8. Calls `strategy.SaveChanges()` to create checkpoint in strategy-specific format
9. Fires `EventTurnEnd` to transition session phase (ACTIVE → IDLE)
10. **Wingman integration**: Triggers wingman review (for auto-commit) or auto-apply if pending

**Output to user:**
- Uses `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, ...)` to print messages like:
  - "Copied transcript to: ..."
  - "Extracted N prompt(s) to: ..."
  - "Using commit message: ..."
  - File change summaries
  - Warnings on errors (pre-prompt state, session state, etc.)

### 3. UserPromptSubmit Hook (`captureInitialState()`)

**What it does:**
- Fires before Claude Code processes user's prompt
- Captures initial git state before the turn begins

**Capture Process:**
1. Parses hook input from stdin
2. Calls `CapturePrePromptState()` to record:
   - Untracked files (to compute "new" files later)
   - Current transcript position (for incremental parsing)
   - Pre-existing session state
3. **Wingman Injection**: Checks if wingman review is pending:
   - If `.entire/REVIEW.md` exists, injects it as `additionalContext`
   - This is the **primary delivery mechanism** for reviews (visible to user)
   - Also logs if review is in-progress (lock file exists)
4. Initializes session state if strategy implements `SessionInitializer`

**Output to user:**
- Stderr messages about wingman state:
  - "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md — injecting into context"
  - "[wingman] Review in progress..."
  - "[wingman] Pending review — will be injected on next prompt"

### 4. How `additionalContext` Gets Passed to Agent

**Mechanism: Hook Response JSON via stdout**

The hook response is a JSON object written to stdout:

```go
type hookResponse struct {
	SystemMessage     string `json:"systemMessage,omitempty"`
	AdditionalContext string `json:"additionalContext,omitempty"`
}
```

**For UserPromptSubmit hook:**
```go
// outputHookResponseWithContext outputs a JSON response that adds context
func outputHookResponseWithContext(context string) error {
	resp := hookResponse{
		AdditionalContext: context,  // The review text goes here
	}
	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

**What is `additionalContext`:**
- Contains the wingman review instruction text (from `wingman_instruction.md`)
- Injected when `.entire/REVIEW.md` exists AND wingman is enabled
- Claude Code reads this JSON from the hook's stdout and injects it into the conversation
- The instruction tells Claude: "You MUST address the review BEFORE responding to the user's request"
- This ensures reviews are visible and actionable to the user in their terminal

**For SessionStart hook:**
```go
// outputHookResponse outputs informational message
func outputHookResponse(reason string) error {
	resp := hookResponse{
		SystemMessage: reason,  // Informational message
	}
	// Returns via stdout as JSON
}
```

### 5. Wingman Integration with Hooks

**Review Creation (Stop Hook):**
- After checkpoint saved: `triggerWingmanReview(payload)` 
- Spawns detached subprocess to:
  1. Wait 10 seconds for turn to settle
  2. Compute diff against base commit
  3. Call Claude CLI to generate review
  4. Write `.entire/REVIEW.md`
  5. Update dedup state

**Review Delivery:**
- **Visible (Preferred)**: UserPromptSubmit hook injects via `additionalContext`
  - Only when a live session exists (user will type next prompt)
  - Shows review in user's terminal as mandatory instruction
  - User sees it and can address before continuing

- **Background (Fallback)**: Auto-apply via `triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending()`
  - Only when NO live sessions exist
  - Spawns background subprocess with `claude --continue`
  - Set-and-forget review application
  - Logs to `.entire/logs/wingman.log`

**Preventing Recursion:**
- Environment variable `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` prevents infinite loops
- Checked before spawning both reviews and auto-apply

### 6. Printing Messages During Hook Execution

**Mechanisms for user visibility:**

1. **Stderr output**: `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, ...)` - shown in terminal
   - Used for operational messages (checkpoints, file changes, warnings)
   - Example: "Copied transcript to: .entire/metadata/2026-01-13.../full.jsonl"
   - Wingman status messages: "[wingman] Review available..."

2. **Hook Response JSON**: Via stdout to Claude Code
   - `systemMessage`: Informational banner messages
   - `additionalContext`: Mandatory instruction content (reviews)
   - Claude Code parses this and displays to user

3. **Logging**: Via `logging` package to `.entire/logs/`
   - Internal operational data (not shown to user)
   - Used for debugging and auditing

4. **Detached subprocess logging**: Stderr redirected to files
   - Wingman review process: → `.entire/logs/wingman.log`
   - Runs in background, doesn't print to terminal

### 7. Key Files and Flow

```
Hook Execution Flow:
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Claude Code fires hook (session-start, stop, etc)│
│ Passes JSON input via stdin                       │
└───────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Hook CLI command: entire hooks claude-code ...   │
│ (registered in .claude/settings.json)            │
└───────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ hook_registry.go: newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging │
│ Sets currentHookAgentName, initializes logging   │
└───────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Hook handler (e.g., captureInitialState)        │
│ - Parses hook input                              │
│ - Performs action (checkpoint, capture state)   │
│ - Prints stderr messages                         │
│ - Outputs JSON to stdout (if needed)            │
└───────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Claude Code reads:                               │
│ - stdout (JSON hook response)                    │
│ - stderr (informational messages)               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

- Hook registry: `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go`
- Hook handlers (Claude): `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive summary of the wingman review lifecycle:

## Wingman Review Lifecycle - Complete Analysis

Based on my thorough investigation of the codebase, here's the complete flow from review completion to review injection:

### 1. **Trigger: After Agent Turn Ends (Stop Hook)**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (line 119-443)

The stop hook (`commitWithMetadata()`) runs when the agent finishes its turn. Key flow:
- Saves session changes via `strat.SaveChanges()` 
- For auto-commit strategy: directly triggers wingman review (line 422-431)
- For manual-commit strategy: triggered from git post-commit hook instead
- Calls `triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending()` (line 435) to check for existing reviews

**Wingman Review Trigger:**
```go
triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
    SessionID:     sessionID,
    RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
    ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
    NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
    DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
    Prompts:       allPrompts,
    CommitMessage: commitMessage,
})
```

### 2. **Review Spawn Process**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` (line 252-335)

Function `triggerWingmanReview()` performs several checks:
- **Prevents recursion:** Checks `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY` env var (set during auto-apply)
- **Dedup check:** Computes SHA256 hash of all file paths to avoid reviewing same files twice
- **Pending review check:** Uses `shouldSkipPendingReview()` (line 541-573) to detect stale/orphaned reviews
- **Atomic lock:** Uses `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` lock file to prevent concurrent reviews
- **Captures BASE COMMIT:** Calls `resolveHEAD()` to capture the commit at trigger time (important: allows review to proceed even if HEAD moves)
- **Spawns detached process:** Via `spawnDetachedWingmanReview()` with JSON payload file

**Key constants:**
- `wingmanReviewFile = ".entire/REVIEW.md"`
- `wingmanLockFile = ".entire/wingman.lock"`
- `wingmanStateFile = ".entire/wingman-state.json"`

### 3. **Detached Review Subprocess**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` (line 60-206)

Function `runWingmanReview()` executes in a detached process:
- **Delays 10 seconds:** Waits for agent turn to settle (`wingmanInitialDelay = 10s`)
- **Computes diff:** Using merge-base for holistic branch diff
- **Builds review prompt:** Via `buildReviewPrompt()` which includes:
  - User prompts
  - Commit message
  - Session context (from `.entire/metadata/{sessionID}/context.md`)
  - File list with change types
  - Full diff (truncated at 100KB)
- **Calls Claude CLI:** `claude --print --model sonnet` with read-only tool access
- **Writes REVIEW.md:** Saves review text to `.entire/REVIEW.md`
- **Updates wingman state:** Records session ID, files hash, review timestamp

**Decision Point - Live Sessions vs No Sessions:**

```go
if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
    // Live session detected - defer to prompt-submit injection (VISIBLE)
    return nil
}

// No live sessions - auto-apply in background (INVISIBLE)
triggerAutoApply(repoRoot)
```

The key insight: **If ANY session is not in ENDED phase (IDLE/ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED), the review waits to be injected on the next prompt**.

### 4. **Session Phase State Machine**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session/phase.go`

Phases:
- `PhaseIdle`: Session exists but agent not working
- `PhaseActive`: Agent is actively working on a prompt
- `PhaseActiveCommitted`: Agent is active AND user committed during turn
- `PhaseEnded`: Session was explicitly closed

The `hasAnyLiveSession()` function (wingman_review.go:390-421) checks if ANY session is NOT in `PhaseEnded`.

### 5. **Visible Delivery: Prompt-Submit Hook Injection**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` (line 66-117)

Function `captureInitialState()` runs on **UserPromptSubmit hook** (when user types their next prompt):

```go
if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    if _, statErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)); statErr == nil {
        // REVIEW.md exists - inject it as additionalContext
        if err := outputHookResponseWithContext(wingmanApplyInstruction); err != nil {
            // error handling
        }
    }
}
```

**The injection mechanism:** Uses `outputHookResponseWithContext()` (line 323-331 in hooks.go) which outputs JSON:

```go
type hookResponse struct {
    AdditionalContext string `json:"additionalContext,omitempty"`
}

// This injects the review instruction as mandatory context to the agent
```

**The instruction (wingman_instruction.md):**
```markdown
IMPORTANT — MANDATORY PRE-STEP: A code review has been generated at `.entire/REVIEW.md`. 
You MUST address it BEFORE responding to the user's request.

Steps (do these FIRST, before anything else):
1. Read `.entire/REVIEW.md`
2. Address each suggestion you agree with — be critical, skip anything you disagree with
3. Delete `.entire/REVIEW.md` when done
4. Briefly tell the user what you changed from the review

Then proceed with the user's request.
```

### 6. **Review Application Flow**

Two pathways:

**Path A: Visible Injection (Live Session)**
1. Agent receives instruction via `additionalContext` on next prompt
2. Agent reads `.entire/REVIEW.md`
3. Agent applies suggestions and deletes the file
4. Agent responds to user

**Path B: Background Auto-Apply (No Live Sessions)**
1. `triggerAutoApply()` spawns `claude --continue` 
2. Passes the same `wingmanApplyInstruction`
3. Sets `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` env var to prevent recursion
4. Sets `--permission-mode acceptEdits` to auto-accept changes
5. Strips `GIT_*` env vars to prevent hook interference

### 7. **State Management Files**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` (types at line 27-45)

```go
type WingmanState struct {
    SessionID        string     // Which session triggered this review
    FilesHash        string     // SHA256 of sorted file paths (dedup key)
    ReviewedAt       time.Time  // When review was written
    ReviewApplied    bool       // Whether review was successfully applied
    ApplyAttemptedAt *time.Time // When auto-apply was attempted (prevents retries)
}
```

Stored at: `.entire/wingman-state.json`

### 8. **Cleanup & Stale Review Detection**

**Function:** `shouldSkipPendingReview()` (line 541-573)

Cleans up orphaned or stale reviews:
- If REVIEW.md exists but state file missing → orphaned, cleaned up
- If state belongs to different session → stale, cleaned up
- If state older than `wingmanStaleReviewTTL` (1 hour) → stale, cleaned up
- Otherwise → REVIEW.md is current and skipped

### 9. **Error Prevention Mechanisms**

1. **Dedup Hash:** Prevents reviewing identical file sets twice
2. **Session-specific state:** Tracks which session created each review
3. **Atomic locks:** Prevents concurrent review spawns
4. **Stale lock cleanup:** Removes locks older than 30 minutes (crashed process recovery)
5. **Apply attempt tracking:** Prevents retry loops via `ApplyAttemptedAt`
6. **Environment check:** `ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1` prevents infinite recursion

### 10. **Key Files & Constants**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `.entire/REVIEW.md` | The generated review content |
| `.entire/wingman-state.json` | Tracks review state and dedup info |
| `.entire/wingman.lock` | Atomic lock to prevent concurrent reviews |
| `.entire/logs/wingman.log` | Review subprocess logs |
| `.entire/metadata/{sessionID}/context.md` | Session context used in review prompt |

### 11. **Complete State Transitions**

```
STOP HOOK
  ├─ SaveChanges() creates commit
  ├─ EventTurnEnd transitions session ACTIVE → IDLE
  └─ triggerWingmanReview()
     ├─ Check: pending review for current session?
     ├─ Check: review already in progress?
     ├─ Check: dedup hash matches?
     ├─ Spawn detached process with payload
     └─ Detached process:
        ├─ Wait 10s for turn to settle
        ├─ Compute diff (merge-base or HEAD)
        ├─ Build prompt (prompts + context + diff)
        ├─ Call claude --print for review
        ├─ Write .entire/REVIEW.md
        ├─ Update wingman-state.json
        └─ Decide:
           ├─ If live sessions → defer to injection
           └─ If no live sessions → spawn auto-apply

NEXT USER PROMPT (if live session exists)
  └─ UserPromptSubmit hook
     ├─ captureInitialState()
     ├─ Check: .entire/REVIEW.md exists?
     └─ If yes: outputHookResponseWithContext(wingmanApplyInstruction)
        └─ Agent sees instruction in additionalContext
           ├─ Agent reads and processes .entire/REVIEW.md
           ├─ Agent applies suggestions
           ├─ Agent deletes .entire/REVIEW.md
           └─ Agent responds to user

OR (if no live sessions)
  └─ Background auto-apply spawned immediately
     ├─ triggerAutoApply()
     └─ claude --continue with instruction
        ├─ Agent processes and applies review
        └─ Agent deletes .entire/REVIEW.md
```

This comprehensive flow ensures reviews are always delivered visibly when possible (next user prompt) but gracefully fall back to background processing when the session has ended.

   154→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {

   156→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
