   189→
   190→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   191→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   192→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   193→	var transcriptOffset int
   194→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   195→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   196→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   197→	} else {
   198→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   199→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   200→		if loadErr != nil {
   201→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   202→		}
   203→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   204→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   205→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   210→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   211→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   212→	var totalLines int
   213→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   214→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   215→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   216→		if err != nil {
   217→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   218→		}
   219→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   220→	} else {
   221→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   222→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   223→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   224→		if err != nil {
   225→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   226→		}
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   230→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   231→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   232→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   233→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   234→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   237→
   238→	// Extract summary
   239→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   240→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   241→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   243→	}
   244→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   245→
   246→	// Get modified files from transcript
   247→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   248→
   249→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   250→	lastPrompt := ""
   251→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   252→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   253→	}
   254→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   255→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   256→
   257→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   258→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   259→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if preState != nil {
   265→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   269→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   275→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   276→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   277→	if changes != nil {
   278→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   279→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   283→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   284→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   285→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   286→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   287→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   288→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   289→		// Auto-apply pending wingman review even when no file changes this turn
   290→		triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   291→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   292→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   293→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   294→		}
   295→		return nil
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   299→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   300→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   301→	}
   302→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   304→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   305→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   306→		}
   307→	}
   308→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   309→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   310→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   311→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   312→		}
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Create context file before saving changes
   316→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   317→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   318→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   319→	}
   320→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   321→
   322→	// Get git author from local/global config
   323→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   324→	if err != nil {
   325→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Get the configured strategy
   329→	strat := GetStrategy()
   330→
   331→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   332→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   333→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   337→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   338→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   339→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   343→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   344→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   345→	if preState != nil {
   346→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   347→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   351→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   352→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   353→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   354→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   355→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   356→		if err != nil {
   357→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   358→		} else {
   359→			tokenUsage = usage
   360→		}
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   364→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   365→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   366→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   367→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   368→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   369→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   370→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   371→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   372→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   373→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   374→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   375→		AgentType:                agentType,
   376→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   377→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   378→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   382→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   386→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   387→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   388→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   389→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   390→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   391→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   392→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   393→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   394→		if loadErr != nil {
   395→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   396→		}
   397→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   398→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   399→		if sessionState == nil {
   400→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   401→				SessionID: sessionID,
   402→			}
   403→		}
   404→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   405→		sessionState.StepCount++
   406→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   407→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   408→		} else {
   409→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   410→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   411→		}
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   415→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   416→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   417→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   418→
   419→	// Trigger wingman review for auto-commit strategy (commit already happened
   420→	// in SaveChanges). Manual-commit triggers wingman from the git post-commit hook
   421→	// instead, since the user commits manually.
   422→	if totalChanges > 0 && strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   423→		triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
   424→			SessionID:     sessionID,
   425→			RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
   426→			ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
   427→			NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
   428→			DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
   429→			Prompts:       allPrompts,
   430→			CommitMessage: commitMessage,
   431→		})
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	// Auto-apply pending wingman review on turn end
   435→	triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot)
   436→
   437→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   438→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   439→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   440→	}
   441→
   442→	return nil
   443→}
   444→
   445→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   446→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   447→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   448→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   449→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   450→	if err != nil {
   451→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Get agent for logging context
   455→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   461→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   462→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   463→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   464→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   465→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   466→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   467→	)
   468→
   469→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   470→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   471→	if !found {
   472→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   473→		return nil
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   477→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   478→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   479→		return nil
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   483→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   484→	if err != nil {
   485→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   490→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   491→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   492→		return nil
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Get git author
   496→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   497→	if err != nil {
   498→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   499→		return nil
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Get the active strategy
   503→	strat := GetStrategy()
   504→
   505→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   506→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   507→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   508→		return nil
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   512→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   513→	if sessionID == "" {
   514→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   518→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   519→
   520→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   521→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   522→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   523→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   524→	if todoContent == "" {
   525→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   526→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   527→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   528→		if todoCount > 0 {
   529→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   530→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   531→		}
   532→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   533→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   537→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   538→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   539→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   543→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   544→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   545→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   546→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   547→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   548→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   549→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   550→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   551→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   552→		IsIncremental:       true,
   553→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   554→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   555→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   556→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   557→		AgentType:           agentType,
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   561→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   562→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   563→		return nil
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   567→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   568→	return nil
   569→}
   570→
   571→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   572→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   573→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   574→	if err != nil {
   575→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   576→	}
   577→
   578→	// Get agent for logging context
   579→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   580→	if err != nil {
   581→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   585→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   586→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   587→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   588→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   589→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   590→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   591→	)
   592→
   593→	// Log context to stdout
   594→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   595→
   596→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   597→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   598→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   599→	// in file changes.
   600→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   601→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	return nil
   605→}
   606→
   607→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   608→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   609→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   610→	if err != nil {
   611→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   612→	}
   613→
   614→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   615→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   616→
   617→	// Get agent for logging context
   618→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	// Log parsed input context
   624→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   625→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   626→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   627→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   628→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   629→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   630→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   631→	)
   632→
   633→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   634→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   635→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   636→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   637→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   638→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   639→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   640→		}
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	// Log context to stdout
   644→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   645→
   646→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   647→	var modifiedFiles []string
   648→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   649→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   650→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   651→		if err != nil {
   652→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   653→		} else {
   654→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   655→		}
   656→	} else {
   657→		// Fall back to main transcript
   658→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   659→		if err != nil {
   660→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   661→		} else {
   662→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   663→		}
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   667→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   668→	if err != nil {
   669→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   670→	}
   671→
   672→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   673→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   674→	if err != nil {
   675→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   679→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   680→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   681→	if err != nil {
   682→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Filter and normalize paths
   686→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   687→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   688→	if changes != nil {
   689→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   690→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   694→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   695→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   696→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   697→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   698→		return nil
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   702→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   703→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   704→
   705→	// Get git author
   706→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   707→	if err != nil {
   708→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   709→	}
   710→
   711→	// Get the configured strategy
   712→	strat := GetStrategy()
   713→
   714→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   715→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   716→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   717→	}
   718→
   719→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   720→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   721→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   722→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   726→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   727→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   728→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   729→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   730→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   731→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   732→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   733→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   734→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   735→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   736→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   737→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   738→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   739→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   740→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   741→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   742→		AgentType:              agentType,
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   746→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   747→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   751→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   752→
   753→	return nil
   754→}
   755→
   756→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   757→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   758→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   759→}
   760→
   761→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   762→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   763→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   764→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   765→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   776→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   777→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   778→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   779→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   780→	)
   781→
   782→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   783→		return nil // No session to update
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   787→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   788→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   789→	}
   790→
   791→	// If a wingman review is pending and this was the last live session,
   792→	// trigger background auto-apply. No more prompts will come from this
   793→	// session, so the prompt-submit injection can't deliver the review.
   794→	triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd()
   795→
   796→	return nil
   797→}
   798→
   799→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   800→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   801→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   802→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   803→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   804→	if loadErr != nil {
   805→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   806→		return
   807→	}
   808→	if turnState == nil {
   809→		return
   810→	}
   811→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   812→
   813→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   814→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   815→		strat := GetStrategy()
   816→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   817→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   818→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   819→			}
   820→		}
   821→	}
   822→
   823→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   824→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   825→	}
   826→}
   827→
   828→// triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending checks for a pending REVIEW.md and spawns
   829→// the auto-apply subprocess if conditions are met. Called from the stop hook
   830→// on every turn end (both with-changes and no-changes paths).
   831→//
   832→// When a live session exists, this is a no-op: the prompt-submit injection
   833→// will deliver the review visibly in the user's terminal instead. Background
   834→// auto-apply is only used when no sessions are alive (all ended).
   835→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyIfPending(repoRoot string) {
   836→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   837→		return
   838→	}
   839→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   840→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   841→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   842→		return
   843→	}
   844→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   845→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   846→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply already attempted, skipping")
   847→		return
   848→	}
   849→	// Don't spawn background auto-apply if a live session exists.
   850→	// The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as additionalContext,
   851→	// which is visible to the user in their terminal.
   852→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   853→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred: live session will handle via injection")
   854→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review pending — will be injected on next prompt\n")
   855→		return
   856→	}
   857→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review found, spawning auto-apply (no live sessions)\n")
   858→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply spawning (no live sessions)",
   859→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   860→	)
   861→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   862→}
   863→
   864→// triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd checks for a pending REVIEW.md after a
   865→// session ends. If no live sessions remain, spawns background auto-apply.
   866→// Called from both Claude Code and Gemini CLI SessionEnd hooks.
   867→func triggerWingmanAutoApplyOnSessionEnd() {
   868→	if !settings.IsWingmanEnabled() || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY") != "" {
   869→		return
   870→	}
   871→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   872→	if err != nil {
   873→		return
   874→	}
   875→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
   876→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   877→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr != nil {
   878→		return
   879→	}
   880→	wingmanState := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   881→	if wingmanState != nil && wingmanState.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   882→		return
   883→	}
   884→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   885→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply deferred on session-end: other live sessions remain")
   886→		return
   887→	}
   888→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Last session ended with pending review, spawning auto-apply\n")
   889→	logging.Info(logCtx, "wingman auto-apply on session-end (no live sessions remain)",
   890→		slog.String("review_path", reviewPath),
   891→	)
   892→	spawnDetachedWingmanApply(repoRoot)
   893→}
   894→
   895→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   896→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   897→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   898→	if err != nil {
   899→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   900→	}
   901→	if state == nil {
   902→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   903→	}
   904→
   905→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   906→
   907→	now := time.Now()
   908→	state.EndedAt = &now
   909→
   910→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   911→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   912→	}
   913→	return nil
   914→}
   915→
   916→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   917→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   918→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   919→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   920→
   921→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   922→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   923→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   924→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   925→//
   926→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   927→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   928→//
   929→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   930→// with whatever data is available.
   931→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   932→	const (
   933→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   934→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   935→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   936→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   937→	)
   938→
   939→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   940→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   941→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   942→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   943→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   944→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   945→			)
   946→			return
   947→		}
   948→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   949→	}
   950→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   951→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   952→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   953→	)
   954→}
   955→
   956→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   957→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   958→// close to hookStartTime.
   959→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   960→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   961→	if err != nil {
   962→		return false
   963→	}
   964→	defer f.Close()
   965→
   966→	// Seek to tail
   967→	info, err := f.Stat()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		return false
   970→	}
   971→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   972→	if offset < 0 {
   973→		offset = 0
   974→	}
   975→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   976→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   977→		return false
   978→	}
   979→
   980→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   981→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   982→	for _, line := range lines {
   983→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   984→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   985→			continue
   986→		}
   987→
   988→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   989→		var entry struct {
   990→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   991→		}
   992→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   993→			continue
   994→		}
   995→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   996→		if err != nil {
   997→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   998→			if err != nil {
   999→				continue
  1000→			}
  1001→		}
  1002→
  1003→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
  1004→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
  1005→			return true
  1006→		}
  1007→	}
  1008→	return false
  1009→}
  1010→

    19→// wingmanLog writes a timestamped log line to stderr. In the detached subprocess,
    20→// stderr is redirected to .entire/logs/wingman.log by the spawner.
    21→func wingmanLog(format string, args ...any) {
    22→	msg := fmt.Sprintf(format, args...)
    23→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "%s [wingman] %s\n", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), msg)
    24→}
    25→
    26→const (
    27→	// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,
    28→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.
    29→	wingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second
    30→
    31→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    32→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    33→
    34→	// wingmanGitTimeout is the timeout for git diff operations.
    35→	wingmanGitTimeout = 60 * time.Second
    36→
    37→	// wingmanReviewTimeout is the timeout for the claude --print review call.
    38→	wingmanReviewTimeout = 5 * time.Minute
    39→
    40→	// wingmanApplyTimeout is the timeout for the claude --continue auto-apply call.
    41→	wingmanApplyTimeout = 15 * time.Minute
    42→)
    43→
    44→// wingmanCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.

    49→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    50→	return &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    "__review",
    52→		Hidden: true,
    53→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    54→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    55→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])

    60→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    61→	wingmanLog("review process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
    62→	wingmanLog("reading payload from %s", payloadPath)
    63→
    64→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    65→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		wingmanLog("ERROR reading payload: %v", err)
    68→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    69→	}
    70→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    71→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    72→
    73→	var payload WingmanPayload
    74→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    75→		wingmanLog("ERROR unmarshaling payload: %v", err)
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    80→	if repoRoot == "" {
    81→		wingmanLog("ERROR repo_root is empty in payload")
    82→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	totalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)
    86→	wingmanLog("session=%s repo=%s base_commit=%s files=%d",
    87→		payload.SessionID, repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)
    88→
    89→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    90→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    91→	defer func() {
    92→		if err := os.Remove(lockPath); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
    93→			wingmanLog("WARNING: failed to remove lock file: %v", err)
    94→		} else {
    95→			wingmanLog("lock file removed")
    96→		}
    97→	}()
    98→
    99→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
   100→	wingmanLog("waiting %s for agent turn to settle", wingmanInitialDelay)
   101→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
   102→
   103→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
   104→	wingmanLog("computing diff against %s", payload.BaseCommit)
   105→	diffStart := time.Now()
   106→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		wingmanLog("ERROR computing diff: %v", err)
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   113→		wingmanLog("no changes found in diff, exiting")
   114→		return nil // No changes to review
   115→	}
   116→	wingmanLog("diff computed: %d bytes in %s", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   117→
   118→	// Build file list for the prompt
   119→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   120→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
   121→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
   122→	}
   123→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   124→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   125→	}
   126→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   127→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   128→	}
   129→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   130→
   131→	// Read session context from checkpoint data (best-effort)
   132→	sessionContext := readSessionContext(repoRoot, payload.SessionID)
   133→	if sessionContext != "" {
   134→		wingmanLog("session context loaded: %d bytes", len(sessionContext))
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Build review prompt
   138→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, payload.CommitMessage, sessionContext, payload.SessionID, fileList, diff)
   139→	wingmanLog("review prompt built: %d bytes", len(prompt))
   140→
   141→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   142→	wingmanLog("calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)
   143→	reviewStart := time.Now()
   144→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		wingmanLog("ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   147→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→	wingmanLog("review received: %d bytes in %s", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   150→
   151→	// Write REVIEW.md
   152→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   153→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   154→		wingmanLog("ERROR creating directory: %v", err)
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   158→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   159→		wingmanLog("ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v", err)
   160→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→	wingmanLog("REVIEW.md written to %s", reviewPath)
   163→
   164→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using
   165→	// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   166→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   167→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   168→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   169→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   170→
   171→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
   172→		SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   173→		FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   174→		ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   175→		ReviewApplied: false,
   176→	})
   177→	wingmanLog("dedup state saved")
   178→
   179→	// If any session is live (IDLE/ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED), don't auto-apply
   180→	// in the background. The prompt-submit hook will inject REVIEW.md as
   181→	// additionalContext when the user sends their next prompt — this is VISIBLE
   182→	// in their terminal. Only use background auto-apply when no sessions are
   183→	// alive (e.g., user closed all sessions).
   184→	if hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot) {
   185→		wingmanLog("live session detected, deferring to prompt-submit injection (visible)")
   186→		return nil
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// No live sessions — apply in background as fallback
   190→	wingmanLog("no live sessions, triggering background auto-apply")
   191→	now := time.Now()
   192→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   193→	if state != nil {
   194→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   195→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	applyStart := time.Now()
   199→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   200→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   204→
   205→	return nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// computeDiff gets the full branch diff for review. It diffs the current HEAD
   209→// against the merge base with the default branch (main/master), giving the
   210→// reviewer a holistic view of all branch changes rather than just one commit.
   211→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, _ string) (string, error) {
   212→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   213→	defer cancel()
   214→
   215→	// Find the merge base with the default branch for a holistic branch diff.
   216→	mergeBase := findMergeBase(ctx, repoRoot)
   217→	if mergeBase != "" {
   218→		wingmanLog("using merge-base %s for branch diff", mergeBase)
   219→		diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, mergeBase)
   220→		if err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(diff) != "" {
   221→			return diff, nil
   222→		}
   223→		// Fall through to HEAD diff if merge-base diff fails or is empty
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Fallback: diff uncommitted changes against HEAD
   227→	wingmanLog("falling back to HEAD diff")
   228→	diff, err := gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD")
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// If no uncommitted changes, try the latest commit itself
   234→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
   235→		diff, err = gitDiff(ctx, repoRoot, "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   236→		if err != nil {
   237→			return "", nil
   238→		}
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	return diff, nil
   242→}
   243→
   244→// findMergeBase returns the merge-base commit between HEAD and the default
   245→// branch (tries main, then master). Returns empty string if not found.
   246→func findMergeBase(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string) string {
   247→	for _, branch := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   248→		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "merge-base", branch, "HEAD") //nolint:gosec // branch is from hardcoded slice
   249→		cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   250→		out, err := cmd.Output()
   251→		if err == nil {
   252→			return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   253→		}
   254→	}
   255→	return ""
   256→}
   257→
   258→// gitDiff runs git diff with the given args and returns stdout.
   259→func gitDiff(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   260→	fullArgs := append([]string{"diff"}, args...)
   261→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", fullArgs...) //nolint:gosec // args are from internal logic
   262→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   263→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   264→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   265→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   266→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   267→		return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff %v: %w (stderr: %s)", args, err, stderr.String())
   268→	}
   269→	return stdout.String(), nil
   270→}
   271→
   272→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   273→// repoRoot is the repository root so the reviewer can access the full codebase.
   274→func callClaudeForReview(repoRoot, prompt string) (string, error) {
   275→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   276→	defer cancel()
   277→
   278→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   279→		"--print",
   280→		"--output-format", "json",
   281→		"--model", wingmanReviewModel,
   282→		"--setting-sources", "",
   283→		// Grant read-only tool access so the reviewer can inspect source files
   284→		// beyond just the diff. Permission bypass is safe here since tools are
   285→		// restricted to read-only operations.
   286→		"--allowedTools", "Read,Glob,Grep",
   287→		"--permission-mode", "bypassPermissions",
   288→	)
   289→
   290→	// Run from repo root so the reviewer can read source files for context.
   291→	// Loop-breaking is handled by --setting-sources "" (disables hooks) and
   292→	// wingmanStripGitEnv (prevents git index pollution).
   293→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   294→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   295→
   296→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   297→
   298→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   299→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   300→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   301→
   302→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   303→		var execErr *exec.Error
   304→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   305→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   306→		}
   307→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   308→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   309→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   310→		}
   311→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Parse the CLI response
   315→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   316→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   317→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   321→}
   322→
   323→// readSessionContext reads the context.md file from the session's checkpoint
   324→// metadata directory. Returns empty string if unavailable.
   325→func readSessionContext(repoRoot, sessionID string) string {
   326→	if sessionID == "" {
   327→		return ""
   328→	}
   329→	contextPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "context.md")
   330→	data, err := os.ReadFile(contextPath) //nolint:gosec // path constructed from repoRoot + session ID
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		return ""
   333→	}
   334→	return string(data)
   335→}
   336→
   337→// isSessionIdle checks if any session is in the IDLE phase by reading
   338→// session state files directly from the repo. Uses repoRoot to locate
   339→// .git/entire-sessions/ without relying on the current working directory
   340→// (which is "/" in detached subprocesses).
   341→func isSessionIdle(repoRoot, sessionID string) bool {
   342→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   343→	if sessDir == "" {
   344→		wingmanLog("cannot find session state dir for %s", repoRoot)
   345→		return false
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Fast path: check the specific session
   349→	if sessionID != "" {
   350→		if phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sessionID); phase == string(session.PhaseIdle) {
   351→			return true
   352→		}
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	// Slow path: check if ANY session is idle
   356→	// (covers session restart, ended sessions, etc.)
   357→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   358→	if err != nil {
   359→		return false
   360→	}
   361→	const maxSessionCheck = 50
   362→	checked := 0
   363→	for _, entry := range entries {
   364→		if checked >= maxSessionCheck {
   365→			wingmanLog("hit session check limit (%d), assuming not idle", maxSessionCheck)
   366→			return false
   367→		}
   368→		name := entry.Name()
   369→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   370→			continue
   371→		}
   372→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   373→		if sid == sessionID {
   374→			continue // Already checked
   375→		}
   376→		checked++
   377→		if phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid); phase == string(session.PhaseIdle) {
   378→			wingmanLog("found idle session %s (original session %s was not idle)", sid, sessionID)
   379→			return true
   380→		}
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	return false
   384→}
   385→
   386→// hasAnyLiveSession checks if any session is in a non-ENDED phase (IDLE,
   387→// ACTIVE, or ACTIVE_COMMITTED). Used to decide whether to defer review
   388→// application to the prompt-submit injection (visible to user) vs background
   389→// auto-apply (invisible).
   390→func hasAnyLiveSession(repoRoot string) bool {
   391→	sessDir := findSessionStateDir(repoRoot)
   392→	if sessDir == "" {
   393→		return false
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(sessDir)
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		return false
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	const maxCheck = 50
   402→	checked := 0
   403→	for _, entry := range entries {
   404→		if checked >= maxCheck {
   405→			return false
   406→		}
   407→		name := entry.Name()
   408→		if !strings.HasSuffix(name, ".json") {
   409→			continue
   410→		}
   411→		checked++
   412→		sid := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".json")
   413→		phase := readSessionPhase(sessDir, sid)
   414→		if phase != "" && phase != string(session.PhaseEnded) {
   415→			wingmanLog("found live session %s (phase=%s)", sid, phase)
   416→			return true
   417→		}
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	return false
   421→}
   422→
   423→// findSessionStateDir locates the .git/entire-sessions/ directory by
   424→// reading .git to handle both normal repos and worktrees.
   425→func findSessionStateDir(repoRoot string) string {
   426→	gitPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".git")
   427→	info, err := os.Stat(gitPath)
   428→	if err != nil {
   429→		return ""
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	var gitDir string
   433→	if info.IsDir() {
   434→		// Normal repo: .git is a directory
   435→		gitDir = gitPath
   436→	} else {
   437→		// Worktree: .git is a file containing "gitdir: <path>"
   438→		data, readErr := os.ReadFile(gitPath) //nolint:gosec // path from repoRoot
   439→		if readErr != nil {
   440→			return ""
   441→		}
   442→		content := strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
   443→		if !strings.HasPrefix(content, "gitdir: ") {
   444→			return ""
   445→		}
   446→		gitDir = strings.TrimPrefix(content, "gitdir: ")
   447→		if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
   448→			gitDir = filepath.Join(repoRoot, gitDir)
   449→		}
   450→		// For worktrees, session state is in the common dir
   451→		// .git/worktrees/<name> → ../../ is the common .git dir
   452→		commonDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "..", "..")
   453→		gitDir = filepath.Clean(commonDir)
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	sessDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "entire-sessions")
   457→	if _, statErr := os.Stat(sessDir); statErr != nil {
   458→		return ""
   459→	}
   460→	return sessDir
   461→}
   462→
   463→// readSessionPhase reads just the phase field from a session state JSON file.
   464→func readSessionPhase(sessDir, sessionID string) string {
   465→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(sessDir, sessionID+".json")) //nolint:gosec // sessDir is from git internals
   466→	if err != nil {
   467→		return ""
   468→	}
   469→	var partial struct {
   470→		Phase string `json:"phase"`
   471→	}
   472→	if json.Unmarshal(data, &partial) != nil {
   473→		return ""
   474→	}
   475→	return partial.Phase
   476→}
   477→
   478→// runWingmanApply is the entrypoint for the __apply subcommand, spawned by the
   479→// stop hook when a pending REVIEW.md is detected. It re-checks preconditions
   480→// and triggers claude --continue to apply the review.
   481→func runWingmanApply(repoRoot string) error {
   482→	wingmanLog("apply process started (pid=%d)", os.Getpid())
   483→
   484→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   485→	if !fileExists(reviewPath) {
   486→		wingmanLog("no REVIEW.md found, nothing to apply")
   487→		return nil
   488→	}
   489→
   490→	// Retry prevention: check if apply was already attempted for this review
   491→	state := loadWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot)
   492→	if state != nil && state.ApplyAttemptedAt != nil {
   493→		wingmanLog("apply already attempted at %s, skipping", state.ApplyAttemptedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   494→		return nil
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	// Re-check session is still idle (user may have typed during spawn delay)
   498→	if state != nil && state.SessionID != "" {
   499→		if !isSessionIdle(repoRoot, state.SessionID) {
   500→			wingmanLog("session became active during spawn, aborting (next stop hook will retry)")
   501→			return nil
   502→		}
   503→	}
   504→
   505→	// Mark apply as attempted BEFORE triggering
   506→	if state != nil {
   507→		now := time.Now()
   508→		state.ApplyAttemptedAt = &now
   509→		saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, state)
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	wingmanLog("triggering auto-apply via claude --continue")
   513→	applyStart := time.Now()
   514→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   515→		wingmanLog("ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)
   516→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   517→	}
   518→	wingmanLog("auto-apply completed in %s", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
   519→
   520→	return nil
   521→}
   522→
   523→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   524→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   525→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   526→	defer cancel()
   527→
   528→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude",
   529→		"--continue",
   530→		"--print",
   531→		"--setting-sources", "",
   532→		// Auto-accept edits so the agent can modify files and delete REVIEW.md
   533→		// without requiring user consent (this runs in a background process).
   534→		"--permission-mode", "acceptEdits",
   535→		wingmanApplyInstruction,
   536→	)
   537→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   538→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, and set
   539→	// ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1 so git hooks (post-commit) know not to
   540→	// trigger another wingman review (prevents infinite recursion).
   541→	env := wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   542→	env = append(env, "ENTIRE_WINGMAN_APPLY=1")
   543→	cmd.Env = env
   544→
   545→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   546→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   547→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   548→
   549→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   550→}
   551→
   552→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   553→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   554→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   555→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   556→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   557→	for _, e := range env {
   558→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   559→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   560→		}
   561→	}
   562→	return filtered
   563→}
   564→
   565→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   566→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   567→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   568→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   569→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   570→		return
   571→	}
   572→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   573→	if err != nil {
   574→		return
   575→	}
   576→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   577→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   578→}

   158→// ExtractTodoContentFromToolInput extracts the content of the in-progress todo item from TodoWrite tool_input.
   159→// Falls back to the first pending item if no in-progress item is found.
   160→// Returns empty string if no suitable item is found or JSON is invalid.
   161→//
   162→// This function unwraps the outer tool_input object to extract the todos array,
   163→// then delegates to strategy.ExtractInProgressTodo for the actual parsing logic.
   164→func ExtractTodoContentFromToolInput(toolInput json.RawMessage) string {
   165→	if len(toolInput) == 0 {

   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// First extract the todos array from tool_input
   170→	var input todoWriteToolInput
   171→	if err := json.Unmarshal(toolInput, &input); err != nil {
   172→		return ""
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Delegate to strategy package for the actual extraction logic
   176→	return strategy.ExtractInProgressTodo(input.Todos)
   177→}
   178→
   179→// ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput extracts the content of the last completed todo item.
   180→// In PostToolUse[TodoWrite], the tool_input contains the NEW todo list where the
   181→// just-finished work is marked as "completed". The last completed item represents
   182→// the work that was just done.
   183→//
   184→// Returns empty string if no completed items exist or JSON is invalid.
   185→func ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(toolInput json.RawMessage) string {
   186→	if len(toolInput) == 0 {
   187→		return ""
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// First extract the todos array from tool_input
   191→	var input todoWriteToolInput
   192→	if err := json.Unmarshal(toolInput, &input); err != nil {
   193→		return ""
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Delegate to strategy package for the actual extraction logic
   197→	return strategy.ExtractLastCompletedTodo(input.Todos)
   198→}
   199→
   200→// CountTodosFromToolInput returns the number of todo items in the TodoWrite tool_input.
   201→// Returns 0 if the JSON is invalid or empty.
   202→//
   203→// This function unwraps the outer tool_input object to extract the todos array,
   204→// then delegates to strategy.CountTodos for the actual count.
   205→func CountTodosFromToolInput(toolInput json.RawMessage) int {
   206→	if len(toolInput) == 0 {
   207→		return 0
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// First extract the todos array from tool_input
   211→	var input todoWriteToolInput
   212→	if err := json.Unmarshal(toolInput, &input); err != nil {
   213→		return 0
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Delegate to strategy package for the actual count
   217→	return strategy.CountTodos(input.Todos)
   218→}
   219→
   220→// logPostTaskHookContext logs the PostToolUse[Task] hook context to the writer
   221→func logPostTaskHookContext(w io.Writer, input *PostTaskHookInput, subagentTranscriptPath string) {
   222→	_, _ = fmt.Fprintln(w, "[entire] PostToolUse[Task] hook invoked")
   223→	_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Session ID: %s\n", input.SessionID)
   224→	_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", input.ToolUseID)
   225→
   226→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   227→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Agent ID: %s\n", input.AgentID)
   228→	} else {
   229→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Agent ID: (none)")
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Transcript: %s\n", input.TranscriptPath)
   233→
   234→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   235→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   236→	} else {
   237→		_, _ = fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Subagent Transcript: (none)")
   238→	}
   239→}
   240→
   241→// handleSessionStartCommon is the shared implementation for session start hooks.
   242→// Used by both Claude Code and Gemini CLI handlers.
   243→func handleSessionStartCommon() error {
   244→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionStart, os.Stdin)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   255→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
   256→		slog.String("hook", "session-start"),
   257→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   258→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   259→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   260→	)
   261→
   262→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   263→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Build informational message
   267→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
   268→
   269→	// Append wingman note if enabled
   270→	if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   271→		message += "\n  Wingman is active: your changes will be automatically reviewed."
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
   275→	strat := GetStrategy()
   276→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
   277→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(input.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
   278→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
   279→		}
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Output informational message using agent-specific format
   283→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
   284→		return err
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
   288→	// This handles ENDED → IDLE (re-entering a session).
   289→	// TODO(ENT-221): dispatch ActionWarnStaleSession for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions.
   290→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(input.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
   291→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
   292→	} else if state != nil {
   293→		strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{})
   294→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   295→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	return nil
   300→}
   301→
   302→// hookResponse represents a JSON response.
   303→// Used to control whether Agent continues processing the prompt.
   304→type hookResponse struct {
   305→	SystemMessage     string `json:"systemMessage,omitempty"`
   306→	AdditionalContext string `json:"additionalContext,omitempty"`
   307→}
   308→
   309→// outputHookResponse outputs a JSON response to stdout
   310→func outputHookResponse(reason string) error {
   311→	resp := hookResponse{
   312→		SystemMessage: reason,
   313→	}
   314→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
   315→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
   316→	}
   317→	return nil
   318→}
   319→
   320→// outputHookResponseWithContext outputs a JSON response that adds context to
   321→// the agent's conversation. additionalContext is injected into Claude's context
   322→// and treated as required instructions, not just a warning.
   323→func outputHookResponseWithContext(context string) error {
   324→	resp := hookResponse{
   325→		AdditionalContext: context,
   326→	}
   327→	if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(resp); err != nil {
   328→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode hook response: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→	return nil
   331→}
   332→

   129→		sessionEndCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-end"

   131→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   132→		preTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"

   187→	if count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {

   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Marshal hooks and update raw settings
   192→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)

   195→	}
   196→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   197→
   198→	// Marshal permissions and update raw settings

   200→	if err != nil {

   202→	}
   203→	rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON

   208→	}

   211→	if err != nil {

   213→	}
   214→
   215→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return count, nil
   220→}
   221→
   222→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   223→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   224→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   225→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   228→	}
   229→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   230→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   231→	if err != nil {
   232→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   236→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   241→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   242→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
   243→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   244→		}
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   248→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   249→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   250→	settings.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Stop)
   251→	settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit)
   252→	settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse)
   253→	settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse)
   254→
   255→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   256→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   257→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   258→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   259→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   260→			rawPermissions = nil
   261→		}
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   265→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   266→			var denyRules []string
   267→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   268→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   269→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   270→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   271→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   272→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   273→					}
   274→				}
   275→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   276→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   277→					if err == nil {
   278→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   279→					}
   280→				} else {
   281→					// Remove empty deny array
   282→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   283→				}
   284→			}
   285→		}
   286→
   287→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   288→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   289→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   290→			if err == nil {
   291→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   292→			}
   293→		} else {
   294→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Marshal hooks back
   299→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   304→
   305→	// Write back
   306→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   311→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   312→	}
   313→	return nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   317→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   318→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   319→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   322→	}
   323→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   324→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		return false
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   330→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   331→		return false
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   335→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   336→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   337→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   338→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   339→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   340→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   341→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   342→}
   343→
   344→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   345→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   346→	return []agent.HookType{
   347→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   348→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   349→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   350→		agent.HookStop,
   351→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   352→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   353→	}
   354→}
   355→
   356→// Helper functions for hook management
   357→
   358→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   359→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   360→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   361→			if hook.Command == command {
   362→				return true
   363→			}
   364→		}
   365→	}
   366→	return false
   367→}
   368→
   369→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   370→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   371→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   372→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   373→				if hook.Command == command {
   374→					return true
   375→				}
   376→			}
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→	return false
   380→}
   381→
   382→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   383→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   384→		Type:    "command",
   385→		Command: command,
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   389→	if matcherName == "" {
   390→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   391→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   392→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   393→				return matchers
   394→			}
   395→		}
   396→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   397→			Matcher: "",
   398→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   399→		})
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   403→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   404→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   405→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   406→			return matchers
   407→		}
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   411→		Matcher: matcherName,
   412→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   413→	})
   414→}
   415→
   416→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   417→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   418→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   419→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   420→			return true
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→	return false
   424→}
   425→
   426→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   427→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   428→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   431→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   432→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   433→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   434→			}
   435→		}
   436→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   437→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   438→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   439→			result = append(result, matcher)
   440→		}
   441→	}
   442→	return result
   443→}
   444→
   445→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   446→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   447→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   448→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   449→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   450→}
   451→
```

Based on the search results, here's what I found about the Claude Code hooks `additionalContext` and `UserPromptSubmit` hook response format:

## UserPromptSubmit Hook Response Format

For UserPromptSubmit hooks, the response format includes "decision" (which can be "block" or undefined), "reason" (explanation for decision), and "hookSpecificOutput" containing "hookEventName" and "additionalContext".

Specifically for UserPromptSubmit:

The "hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext" adds the string to the context if not blocked.

### Example Response Structure

A UserPromptSubmit hook response looks like: { "decision": "block" | undefined, "reason": "Explanation for decision", "hookSpecificOutput": { "hookEventName": "UserPromptSubmit", "additionalContext": "My additional context here" } }

### Usage of additionalContext

For UserPromptSubmit hooks, use additionalContext instead to inject text into Claude's context.

UserPromptSubmit and SessionStart can inject text or structured additionalContext into the conversation before Claude proceeds.

### Output Methods

For UserPromptSubmit hooks, you can inject context using either method: Exit code 0 with stdout (Claude sees the context as a special case for UserPromptSubmit) or JSON output (provides more control over the behavior).


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

> ## Documentation Index
